# Every physical skill entry and its disposition

Completed 2026-10-10. 1,316 physical entries are copies/variants, not 1,316 capabilities. 74 historical snapshots are inventory-only; 1,242 current/inactive/fork entries have semantic dispositions from complete reads or verified reviewed bases.

All current byte hashes verified. No installed skill/configuration was changed. Native selection, live workflows and all support payloads remain untested. See [ranked findings](SUMMARY.md), [coverage](coverage.json), [machine ledger](all-skill-dispositions.json) and [independent review](PARENT-REVIEW.md).
